# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

Adds a new Flight to the airline’s managed flights collection.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary:
Creates a reservation for a passenger on a given flight and adds it to the booking.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null, passenger is not null, and now is valid.
2. Ensure the flight is open for booking.
3. Ensure now is before f.departureTime.
4. Ensure passenger is not already reserved on f.
5. Create a new Passenger object using passenger name if needed.
6. Create a new Reservation with a unique id, status PENDING, flight f, and the passenger.
7. Add the reservation to reservations.
8. Also add the reservation to f.reservations.
9. Return true.
10. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- customer : Customer
- reservations : List<Reservation>
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.reservations
- Reservation.id
- Reservation.status
- Reservation.flight
- Reservation.passenger
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- f: the target Flight.
- passenger: passenger name; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the reservation is created successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- customer is not null.
- f is not null and open for booking.
- passenger is a non-empty string.
- now is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- On success, a new Reservation exists in both booking.reservations and f.reservations.
- The new Reservation has a unique id and status PENDING.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

```plantuml
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean

class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary:
Creates a reservation for a passenger on a given flight and adds it to the booking.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null, passenger is not null, and now is valid.
2. Ensure the flight is open for booking.
3. Ensure now is before f.departureTime.
4. Ensure passenger is not already reserved on f.
5. Create a new Passenger object using passenger name if needed.
6. Create a new Reservation with a unique id, status PENDING, flight f, and the passenger.
7. Add the reservation to reservations.
8. Also add the reservation to f.reservations.
9. Return true.
10. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- customer : Customer
- reservations : List<Reservation>
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.reservations
- Reservation.id
- Reservation.status
- Reservation.flight
- Reservation.passenger
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- f: the target Flight.
- passenger: passenger name; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the reservation is created successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- customer is not null.
- f is not null and open for booking.
- passenger is a non-empty string.
- now is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- On success, a new Reservation exists in both booking.reservations and f.reservations.
- The new Reservation has a unique id and status PENDING.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Airport::addCity
Summary:
Associates a city with the airport as one of the cities it serves.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that c is not null.
2. Check whether c already exists in servesForCities.
3. If not present, append c to servesForCities.
4. Otherwise, do nothing.
Related Features:
- id : String
- servesForCities : List<City>
Related Classes: City
Input:
- c: the City to add to the airport’s served-cities list.
Output:
- void.
Pre-condition:
- c is not null.
- The airport object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- If c was not already present, servesForCities contains c exactly once.
- If c was already present, servesForCities remains unchanged.

# Operation Specifications
Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary:
Returns all reservations for this flight whose status is CONFIRMED.
Algorithm:
1. Iterate over reservations.
2. Collect reservations whose status equals CONFIRMED.
3. Return the collected list.
Related Features:
- reservations : List<Reservation>
- Reservation.status
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
- None.
Output:
- List<Reservation>: all confirmed reservations for this flight; empty list if none exist.
Pre-condition:
- The flight object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- The returned list includes only reservations with status CONFIRMED from this flight.
- The flight state is unchanged.

Flight::removeStopover
Summary:
Removes a stopover from the flight.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that stop and now are not null.
2. Check whether stop exists in stopovers.
3. If found, remove it and return true.
4. Otherwise, return false.
Related Features:
- stopovers : List<Stopover>
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input:
- stop: the Stopover to remove.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate.
Output:
- boolean: true if the stopover is removed successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- stop is not null.
- The flight object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- On success, stop is no longer contained in stopovers.
- On failure, stopovers remains unchanged.

Flight::addStopover
Summary:
Adds a stopover to the flight.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that stop and now are not null.
2. Validate that the flight is open for booking, if modification is only allowed before closure.
3. Validate stop.airport is defined.
4. Validate temporal consistency:
   - departureTime < stop.departureTime < stop.arrivalTime < arrivalTime
   - stop times do not overlap existing stopovers.
5. If valid, append stop to stopovers.
6. Return true; otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- departureTime : EDate
- arrivalTime : EDate
- openForBooking : boolean
- stopovers : List<Stopover>
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport, EDate
Input:
- stop: the Stopover to add.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate.
Output:
- boolean: true if the stopover is added successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- stop is not null.
- stop.airport is not null.
- stop.departureTime and stop.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
Post-condition:
- On success, stop is contained in stopovers.
- On failure, stopovers remains unchanged.

# Output Format
Put the revised specifications in a single block as illustrated below:

```result

class_1::operation_1
/*
 * revised description for operation_1
 */

class_1::operation_2
/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */

...
```

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary:
Returns all reservations for this flight whose status is CONFIRMED.
Algorithm:
1. Iterate over reservations.
2. Collect reservations whose status equals CONFIRMED.
3. Return the collected list.
Related Features:
- reservations : List<Reservation>
- Reservation.status
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
- None.
Output:
- List<Reservation>: all confirmed reservations for this flight; empty list if none exist.
Pre-condition:
- The flight object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- The returned list includes only reservations with status CONFIRMED from this flight.
- The flight state is unchanged.

Flight::removeStopover
Summary:
Removes a stopover from the flight.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that stop and now are not null.
2. Check whether stop exists in stopovers.
3. If found, remove it and return true.
4. Otherwise, return false.
Related Features:
- stopovers : List<Stopover>
Related Classes: Stopover, EDate
Input:
- stop: the Stopover to remove.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate.
Output:
- boolean: true if the stopover is removed successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- stop is not null.
- The flight object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- On success, stop is no longer contained in stopovers.
- On failure, stopovers remains unchanged.

Flight::addStopover
Summary:
Adds a stopover to the flight.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that stop and now are not null.
2. Validate that the flight is open for booking, if modification is only allowed before closure.
3. Validate stop.airport is defined.
4. Validate temporal consistency:
   - departureTime < stop.departureTime < stop.arrivalTime < arrivalTime
   - stop times do not overlap existing stopovers.
5. If valid, append stop to stopovers.
6. Return true; otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- departureTime : EDate
- arrivalTime : EDate
- openForBooking : boolean
- stopovers : List<Stopover>
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport, EDate
Input:
- stop: the Stopover to add.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate.
Output:
- boolean: true if the stopover is added successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- stop is not null.
- stop.airport is not null.
- stop.departureTime and stop.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
Post-condition:
- On success, stop is contained in stopovers.
- On failure, stopovers remains unchanged.

# Operation Specifications
Airline::addFlight
Summary:
Adds a new Flight to the airline’s managed flights collection.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.
2. Check whether a flight with the same id already exists in flights.
3. If no duplicate exists, append f to flights.
4. Otherwise, do nothing.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
Related Classes: Flight
Input:
- f: the Flight to be added.
- f.id must be non-empty and unique within the airline.
Output:
- void.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null.
- f.id is defined.
- The airline object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- If no existing flight has the same id, flights contains f exactly once.
- If a duplicate id exists, flights remains unchanged.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary:
Removes a flight from the airline’s managed flights collection.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.
2. Locate f in flights by reference or by matching id.
3. If found, remove it from flights.
4. Otherwise, do nothing.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
Related Classes: Flight
Input:
- f: the Flight to remove.
Output:
- void.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null.
- The airline object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- If f exists in flights, it is removed.
- If f does not exist, flights remains unchanged.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary:
Closes an existing flight that has not yet departed and cancels all confirmed reservations on it.
Algorithm:
1. Find the flight in flights using flightId.
2. If not found, return false.
3. Validate that now is before the flight’s departure date/time.
4. Validate that closing is not performed on the same calendar day as departure.
5. Validate that the flight is currently openForBooking == true.
6. Set openForBooking to false.
7. For each reservation in the flight:
   - if status == CONFIRMED, set it to CANCELED.
8. Return true.
9. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.id
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.reservations
- Reservation.status
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- flightId: identifier of the flight to close; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the flight is successfully closed and confirmed reservations are canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- flightId refers to an existing Flight in flights.
- now is a valid EDate instance/string representation in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Post-condition:
- On success, the flight’s openForBooking becomes false.
- All previously CONFIRMED reservations of the flight become CANCELED.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary:
Searches for flights matching origin, destination, and departure date.
Algorithm:
1. Validate origin, dest, and date.
2. Iterate over flights.
3. Select flights where:
   - departureAirport serves the origin city or its airport is associated with the origin criterion as defined by the caller’s string matching convention,
   - arrivalAirport serves the destination city or matches dest,
   - the flight departure date equals date.
4. Return the matching flights as a list.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.departureAirport
- Flight.arrivalAirport
- Flight.departureTime
- Airport.servesForCities
- City
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
Input:
- origin: origin city or airport descriptor as String.
- dest: destination city or airport descriptor as String.
- date: departure date filter; EDate.
Output:
- List<Flight>: flights satisfying the search criteria; empty list if none match.
Pre-condition:
- origin and dest are non-empty strings.
- date is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- Returned list contains only flights from flights that satisfy the filter.
- The airline state is unchanged.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary:
Publishes a newly created flight for booking.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.
2. Validate departureTime and arrivalTime are present and correctly formatted as yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.
3. Validate temporal consistency:
   - now < departureTime
   - departureTime < arrivalTime
4. Validate route integrity:
   - departureAirport != arrivalAirport
5. Validate that f.openForBooking == false.
6. Validate that the flight has not been published before.
7. Set f.openForBooking to true.
8. Add f to flights if it is not already managed by the airline.
9. Return true.
10. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.arrivalTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.departureAirport
- Flight.arrivalAirport
- Flight.id
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
- f: the Flight to be published.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if publication succeeds and the flight becomes available for booking; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null.
- f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
- f.departureAirport and f.arrivalAirport are defined.
Post-condition:
- On success, f.openForBooking is true.
- The flight is present in flights and is available for customer booking.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.
2. Locate f in flights by reference or by matching id.
3. If found, remove it from flights.
4. Otherwise, do nothing.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
Related Classes: Flight
Input:
- f: the Flight to remove.
Output:
- void.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null.
- The airline object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- If f exists in flights, it is removed.
- If f does not exist, flights remains unchanged.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary:
Closes an existing flight that has not yet departed and cancels all confirmed reservations on it.
Algorithm:
1. Find the flight in flights using flightId.
2. If not found, return false.
3. Validate that now is before the flight’s departure date/time.
4. Validate that closing is not performed on the same calendar day as departure.
5. Validate that the flight is currently openForBooking == true.
6. Set openForBooking to false.
7. For each reservation in the flight:
   - if status == CONFIRMED, set it to CANCELED.
8. Return true.
9. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.id
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.reservations
- Reservation.status
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- flightId: identifier of the flight to close; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the flight is successfully closed and confirmed reservations are canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- flightId refers to an existing Flight in flights.
- now is a valid EDate instance/string representation in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Post-condition:
- On success, the flight’s openForBooking becomes false.
- All previously CONFIRMED reservations of the flight become CANCELED.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary:
Searches for flights matching origin, destination, and departure date.
Algorithm:
1. Validate origin, dest, and date.
2. Iterate over flights.
3. Select flights where:
   - departureAirport serves the origin city or its airport is associated with the origin criterion as defined by the caller’s string matching convention,
   - arrivalAirport serves the destination city or matches dest,
   - the flight departure date equals date.
4. Return the matching flights as a list.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.departureAirport
- Flight.arrivalAirport
- Flight.departureTime
- Airport.servesForCities
- City
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, City, EDate
Input:
- origin: origin city or airport descriptor as String.
- dest: destination city or airport descriptor as String.
- date: departure date filter; EDate.
Output:
- List<Flight>: flights satisfying the search criteria; empty list if none match.
Pre-condition:
- origin and dest are non-empty strings.
- date is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- Returned list contains only flights from flights that satisfy the filter.
- The airline state is unchanged.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary:
Publishes a newly created flight for booking.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null.
2. Validate departureTime and arrivalTime are present and correctly formatted as yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.
3. Validate temporal consistency:
   - now < departureTime
   - departureTime < arrivalTime
4. Validate route integrity:
   - departureAirport != arrivalAirport
5. Validate that f.openForBooking == false.
6. Validate that the flight has not been published before.
7. Set f.openForBooking to true.
8. Add f to flights if it is not already managed by the airline.
9. Return true.
10. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- flights : List<Flight>
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.arrivalTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.departureAirport
- Flight.arrivalAirport
- Flight.id
Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
Input:
- f: the Flight to be published.
- now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if publication succeeds and the flight becomes available for booking; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null.
- f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
- f.departureAirport and f.arrivalAirport are defined.
Post-condition:
- On success, f.openForBooking is true.
- The flight is present in flights and is available for customer booking.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>

    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean

}

}

    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport

    stopovers : List<Stopover>

    flight : Flight

}

    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations  'opposite: flight
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight  'opposite: reservations
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::cancel
Summary:
Cancels an existing reservation identified by reservationID.
Algorithm:
1. Validate reservationID and now.
2. Search all bookings of this customer for a reservation with the given id.
3. If not found, return false.
4. Validate that the associated flight has not departed yet.
5. Validate that the flight is still open for booking.
6. Set the reservation status to CANCELED.
7. Return true.
8. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- bookings : List<Booking>
- Booking.reservations
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Reservation.id
- Reservation.status
- Reservation.flight
Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- reservationID: unique reservation identifier; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the reservation is canceled successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- customer is not null.
- reservationID is not empty.
- now is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- On success, the target reservation status becomes CANCELED.
- No other reservation is modified.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

Customer::confirm
Summary:
Confirms an existing reservation identified by reservationID.
Algorithm:
1. Validate reservationID and now.
2. Search all bookings of this customer for a reservation with the given id.
3. If not found, return false.
4. Validate that the associated flight has not departed yet.
5. Validate that the flight is still open for booking.
6. Set the reservation status to CONFIRMED.
7. Return true.
8. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- bookings : List<Booking>
- Booking.reservations
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.openForBooking
- Reservation.id
- Reservation.status
- Reservation.flight
Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- reservationID: unique reservation identifier; non-empty String.
- now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output:
- boolean: true if the reservation is confirmed successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- customer is not null.
- reservationID is not empty.
- now is a valid EDate value.
Post-condition:
- On success, the target reservation status becomes CONFIRMED.
- No other reservation is modified.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

Customer::addBooking
Summary:
Creates a booking for this customer on a specific flight for a list of passengers.
Algorithm:
1. Validate that f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null, and now is valid.
2. Validate that the flight is open for booking and now is before the flight departure time.
3. Validate that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicate names.
4. Validate that none of the passengers in the list already has a reservation on the same flight.
5. Create a new Booking and link it to this customer.
6. For each passenger name:
   - create a reservation with unique id in PENDING status for flight f.
   - add it to the booking and the flight.
7. Add the booking to bookings.
8. Return true.
9. If any validation fails, return false.
Related Features:
- bookings : List<Booking>
- Booking.customer
- Booking.reservations
- Flight.openForBooking
- Flight.departureTime
- Flight.reservations
- Reservation.id
- Reservation.status
- Reservation.flight
- Reservation.passenger
Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus, EDate
Input:
- f: the flight on which to create the booking.
- now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
- listOfPassengerNames: list of passenger names; each entry is a non-empty String.
Output:
- boolean: true if the booking and all reservations are created successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition:
- f is not null and open for booking.
- now is valid and earlier than f.departureTime.
- listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
- The customer object is initialized.
Post-condition:
- On success, customer.bookings contains one new Booking for f.
- The new Booking contains one Reservation per passenger name, all with status PENDING.
- The flight’s reservations list is updated consistently.
- On failure, no state change occurs.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary:
```
```java
   * Adds a stopover to the flight.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate that stop and now are not null.
   * 2. Validate that the flight is open for booking, if modification is only allowed before closure.
   * 3. Validate stop.airport is defined.
   * 4. Validate temporal consistency:
   *    - departureTime < stop.departureTime < stop.arrivalTime < arrivalTime
   *    - stop times do not overlap existing stopovers.

   * - stop is not null.
   * - stop.airport is not null.
   * - stop.departureTime and stop.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, stop is contained in stopovers.
```

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean addStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    if (stop == null || now == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (stop.getAirport() == null || getDepartureTime() == null || getArrivalTime() == null
        || stop.getDepartureTime() == null || stop.getArrivalTime() == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!(getDepartureTime().before(stop.getDepartureTime())
        && stop.getDepartureTime().before(stop.getArrivalTime())
        && stop.getArrivalTime().before(getArrivalTime())))
    {
      return false;
    }

    for (Stopover existing : getStopovers())
    {
      Date existingDeparture = existing.getDepartureTime();
      Date existingArrival = existing.getArrivalTime();
      if (existingDeparture == null || existingArrival == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (stop.getDepartureTime().before(existingArrival) && existingDeparture.before(stop.getArrivalTime()))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return getStopovers().add(stop);
    
  }

  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    if (customer == null || f == null || passenger == null || passenger.trim().isEmpty() || now == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    if (!f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }

    Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    if (departureTime == null || !now.before(departureTime))
    {
      return false;
    }

    for (Reservation r : f.getReservations())
    {
      Passenger p = r.getPassenger();
      if (p != null && passenger.equals(p.getName()))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(passenger);

    Reservation newReservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();

    newReservation.setId(uniqueId);

    newReservation.setFlight(f);
    newReservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);

    getReservations().add(newReservation);
    f.getReservations().add(newReservation);

```java
   * Creates a booking for this customer on a specific flight for a list of passengers.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate that f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null, and now is valid.
   * 2. Validate that the flight is open for booking and now is before the flight departure time.
   * 3. Validate that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicate names.

   * 8. Return true.
   * 9. If any validation fails, return false.
```

```java
   * 1. Validate that f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null, and now is valid.
   * 2. Validate that the flight is open for booking and now is before the flight departure time.
   * 3. Validate that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicate names.
   * 4. Validate that none of the passengers in the list already has a reservation on the same flight.
   * 5. Create a new Booking and link it to this customer.
   * 6. For each passenger name:
   *    - create a reservation with unique id in PENDING status for flight f.
   *    - add it to the booking and the flight.
   * 7. Add the booking to bookings.
   * 8. Return true.
   * 9. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - bookings : List<Booking>
   * - Booking.customer
   * - Booking.reservations
   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Flight.departureTime
   * - Flight.reservations
   * - Reservation.id
   * - Reservation.status
   * - Reservation.flight
   * - Reservation.passenger
   * Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus, EDate
   * Input:
   * - f: the flight on which to create the booking.
   * - now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * - listOfPassengerNames: list of passenger names; each entry is a non-empty String.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if the booking and all reservations are created successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - f is not null and open for booking.
   * - now is valid and earlier than f.departureTime.
   * - listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * - The customer object is initialized.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, customer.bookings contains one new Booking for f.
   * - The new Booking contains one Reservation per passenger name, all with status PENDING.
   * - The flight’s reservations list is updated consistently.
   * - On failure, no state change occurs.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
```

  3.3 You should not change the method signature;

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Creates a booking for this customer on a specific flight for a list of passengers.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate that f is not null, listOfPassengerNames is not null, and now is valid.
   * 2. Validate that the flight is open for booking and now is before the flight departure time.
   * 3. Validate that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicate names.
   * 4. Validate that none of the passengers in the list already has a reservation on the same flight.
   * 5. Create a new Booking and link it to this customer.
   * 6. For each passenger name:
   *    - create a reservation with unique id in PENDING status for flight f.
   *    - add it to the booking and the flight.
   * 7. Add the booking to bookings.
   * 8. Return true.
   * 9. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - bookings : List<Booking>
   * - Booking.customer
   * - Booking.reservations
   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Flight.departureTime
   * - Flight.reservations
   * - Reservation.id
   * - Reservation.status
   * - Reservation.flight
   * - Reservation.passenger
   * Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus, EDate
   * Input:
   * - f: the flight on which to create the booking.
   * - now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * - listOfPassengerNames: list of passenger names; each entry is a non-empty String.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if the booking and all reservations are created successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - f is not null and open for booking.
   * - now is valid and earlier than f.departureTime.
   * - listOfPassengerNames is not null and contains at least one name.
   * - The customer object is initialized.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, customer.bookings contains one new Booking for f.
   * - The new Booking contains one Reservation per passenger name, all with status PENDING.
   * - The flight’s reservations list is updated consistently.
   * - On failure, no state change occurs.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    if (f == null || now == null || listOfPassengerNames == null || listOfPassengerNames.isEmpty()) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!now.before(f.getDepartureTime())) {
      return false;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < listOfPassengerNames.size(); i++) {
      String name = listOfPassengerNames.get(i);
      if (name == null || name.length() == 0) {
        return false;
      }
      for (int j = i + 1; j < listOfPassengerNames.size(); j++) {
        if (name.equals(listOfPassengerNames.get(j))) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    for (Booking booking : getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservation.getFlight() == f) {
          Passenger passenger = reservation.getPassenger();
          if (passenger != null) {
            String passengerName = passenger.getName();
            for (String candidate : listOfPassengerNames) {
              if (candidate.equals(passengerName)) {
                return false;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    Booking booking = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    booking.setCustomer(this);

    int counter = 1;
    for (String passengerName : listOfPassengerNames) {
      Passenger passenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
      passenger.setName(passengerName);

      Reservation reservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
      reservation.setId(f.getId() + "-" + passengerName + "-" + counter++);
      reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
      reservation.setPassenger(passenger);
      reservation.setFlight(f);

      booking.getReservations().add(reservation);
      f.getReservations().add(reservation);
    }

    getBookings().add(booking);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Confirms an existing reservation identified by reservationID.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate reservationID and now.
   * 2. Search all bookings of this customer for a reservation with the given id.
   * 3. If not found, return false.
   * 4. Validate that the associated flight has not departed yet.
   * 5. Validate that the flight is still open for booking.
   * 6. Set the reservation status to CONFIRMED.
   * 7. Return true.
   * 8. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - bookings : List<Booking>
   * - Booking.reservations
   * - Flight.departureTime
   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Reservation.id
   * - Reservation.status
   * - Reservation.flight
   * Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
   * Input:
   * - reservationID: unique reservation identifier; non-empty String.
   * - now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if the reservation is confirmed successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - customer is not null.
   * - reservationID is not empty.
   * - now is a valid EDate value.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, the target reservation status becomes CONFIRMED.
   * - No other reservation is modified.
   * - On failure, no state change occurs.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    if (reservationID == null || reservationID.length() == 0 || now == null) {
      return false;
    }

    for (Booking booking : getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(reservation.getId())) {
          Flight flight = reservation.getFlight();
          if (flight == null || flight.getDepartureTime() == null) {
            return false;
          }
          if (now.compareTo(flight.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          if (!flight.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Cancels an existing reservation identified by reservationID.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate reservationID and now.
   * 2. Search all bookings of this customer for a reservation with the given id.
   * 3. If not found, return false.
   * 4. Validate that the associated flight has not departed yet.
   * 5. Validate that the flight is still open for booking.
   * 6. Set the reservation status to CANCELED.
   * 7. Return true.
   * 8. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - bookings : List<Booking>
   * - Booking.reservations

   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Reservation.id

   * - Reservation.flight
   * Related Classes: Booking, Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
   * Input:
   * - reservationID: unique reservation identifier; non-empty String.
   * - now: current date-time; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if the reservation is canceled successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - customer is not null.
   * - reservationID is not empty.
   * - now is a valid EDate value.
   * Post-condition:

   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    if (reservationID == null || reservationID.length() == 0 || now == null) {
      return false;
    }

    for (Booking booking : getBookings()) {
      for (Reservation reservation : booking.getReservations()) {
        if (reservationID.equals(reservation.getId())) {
          Flight flight = reservation.getFlight();
          if (flight == null || flight.getDepartureTime() == null) {
            return false;
          }
          if (now.compareTo(flight.getDepartureTime()) >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          if (!flight.isOpenForBooking()) {
            return false;
          }
          reservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public void addFlight(final Flight f)
  {
    if (f == null) {
      return;
    }
    final String id = f.getId();
    if (id == null || id.length() == 0) {
      return;
    }
    for (Flight existing : getFlights()) {
      if (existing == f) {
        return;
      }
      final String existingId = existing.getId();
      if (existingId != null && existingId.equals(id)) {
        return;
      }
    }
    getFlights().add(f);
    
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Publishes a newly created flight for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Validate that f is not null.
   * 2. Validate departureTime and arrivalTime are present and correctly formatted as yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.
   * 3. Validate temporal consistency:
   *    - now < departureTime
   *    - departureTime < arrivalTime
   * 4. Validate route integrity:
   *    - departureAirport != arrivalAirport
   * 5. Validate that f.openForBooking == false.
   * 6. Validate that the flight has not been published before.
   * 7. Set f.openForBooking to true.
   * 8. Add f to flights if it is not already managed by the airline.
   * 9. Return true.
   * 10. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - flights : List<Flight>
   * - Flight.departureTime
   * - Flight.arrivalTime
   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Flight.departureAirport
   * - Flight.arrivalAirport
   * - Flight.id
   * Related Classes: Flight, Airport, EDate
   * Input:
   * - f: the Flight to be published.
   * - now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if publication succeeds and the flight becomes available for booking; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - f is not null.
   * - f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are valid EDate values.
   * - f.departureAirport and f.arrivalAirport are defined.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, f.openForBooking is true.
   * - The flight is present in flights and is available for customer booking.
   * - On failure, no state change occurs.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean publishFlight(final Flight f, final Date now)
  {
    if (f == null || now == null) {
      return false;
    }
    final Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    final Date arrivalTime = f.getArrivalTime();
    if (departureTime == null || arrivalTime == null) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!now.before(departureTime)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!departureTime.before(arrivalTime)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (f.getDepartureAirport() == null || f.getArrivalAirport() == null) {
      return false;
    }
    if (f.getDepartureAirport() == f.getArrivalAirport()) {
      return false;
    }
    if (f.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    if (f.getId() != null) {
      for (Flight existing : getFlights()) {
        if (existing == f) {
          return false;
        }
        if (existing.getId() != null && existing.getId().equals(f.getId())) {
          return false;
        }
      }
    } else {
      return false;
    }
    f.setOpenForBooking(true);
    if (!getFlights().contains(f)) {
      getFlights().add(f);
    }
    return true;
    
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Closes an existing flight that has not yet departed and cancels all confirmed reservations on it.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Find the flight in flights using flightId.
   * 2. If not found, return false.
   * 3. Validate that now is before the flight’s departure date/time.
   * 4. Validate that closing is not performed on the same calendar day as departure.
   * 5. Validate that the flight is currently openForBooking == true.
   * 6. Set openForBooking to false.
   * 7. For each reservation in the flight:
   *    - if status == CONFIRMED, set it to CANCELED.
   * 8. Return true.
   * 9. If any validation fails, return false.
   * Related Features:
   * - flights : List<Flight>
   * - Flight.id
   * - Flight.departureTime
   * - Flight.openForBooking
   * - Flight.reservations
   * - Reservation.status
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus, EDate
   * Input:
   * - flightId: identifier of the flight to close; non-empty String.
   * - now: current date-time used for validation; EDate in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output:
   * - boolean: true if the flight is successfully closed and confirmed reservations are canceled; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - flightId refers to an existing Flight in flights.
   * - now is a valid EDate instance/string representation in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Post-condition:
   * - On success, the flight’s openForBooking becomes false.
   * - All previously CONFIRMED reservations of the flight become CANCELED.
   * - On failure, no state change occurs.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    if (flightId == null || flightId.length() == 0 || now == null) {
      return false;
    }
    Flight target = null;
    for (Flight existing : getFlights()) {
      if (flightId.equals(existing.getId())) {
        target = existing;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (target == null) {
      return false;
    }
    final Date departureTime = target.getDepartureTime();
    if (departureTime == null) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!now.before(departureTime)) {
      return false;
    }
    Calendar c1 = Calendar.getInstance();
    c1.setTime(now);
    Calendar c2 = Calendar.getInstance();
    c2.setTime(departureTime);
    if (c1.get(Calendar.YEAR) == c2.get(Calendar.YEAR)
        && c1.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == c2.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!target.isOpenForBooking()) {
      return false;
    }
    target.setOpenForBooking(false);
    for (Reservation r : target.getReservations()) {
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED) {
        r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }
    return true;
    
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Searches for flights matching origin, destination, and departure date.

   * - origin and dest are non-empty strings.

   * - Returned list contains only flights from flights that satisfy the filter.
   * - The airline state is unchanged.

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    EList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<Flight>();
    if (origin == null || origin.length() == 0 || dest == null || dest.length() == 0 || date == null) {
      return result;
    }
    Calendar dc = Calendar.getInstance();
    dc.setTime(date);
    for (Flight f : getFlights()) {
      if (f.getDepartureTime() == null || f.getDepartureAirport() == null || f.getArrivalAirport() == null) {
        continue;
      }
      Calendar fc = Calendar.getInstance();
      fc.setTime(f.getDepartureTime());
      if (dc.get(Calendar.YEAR) != fc.get(Calendar.YEAR) || dc.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) != fc.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR)) {
        continue;
      }
      boolean originMatch = false;
      if (f.getDepartureAirport().getId() != null && f.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin)) {
        originMatch = true;
      } else {
        for (City c : f.getDepartureAirport().getServesForCities()) {
          if (c != null && origin.equals(c.toString())) {
            originMatch = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      boolean destMatch = false;
      if (f.getArrivalAirport().getId() != null && f.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest)) {
        destMatch = true;
      } else {
        for (City c : f.getArrivalAirport().getServesForCities()) {
          if (c != null && dest.equals(c.toString())) {
            destMatch = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (originMatch && destMatch) {
        result.add(f);
      }
    }
    return result;
    
  }
